# Stage B fast-channel state-family research gate

## Wall that triggered this gate

The source-command Stage 1 clamp authenticated seven independent failures in fast-sodium activation/deactivation and Kv3-like deactivation, so compartment integration and conductance calibration are stopped.

**Why work stopped:** The filed equation-derived gate roots and current-to-gate time conversions preserve some equilibrium endpoints but do not reproduce the complete powered current while activation, inactivation, and recovery states evolve together.

### Failed attempts already counted

- V1 treated source normalized-conductance Boltzmann curves as unpowered gates and was superseded before execution because m^3 and n^4 shifted the measured curves. (research/findings/2026-08-05-v14-stageB-successor-preregistration-audit-CORRECTION.md)
- V2 applied equation-derived roots and conditional current-to-gate time conversions, but seven complete-current source gates failed under the sealed command protocols. (research/findings/2026-08-05-v14-stageB-fast-channel-source-transfer-STRUCTURAL-NO-GO.md)

No further implementation or tuning against this wall is authorized until every open question below is
resolved from primary evidence or explicitly recorded as absent after an external search.

## Unresolved questions

| ID | Kind | Status | Precise question |
|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | parameter | open | Which directly reported transition-rate equations or complete-current model parameters reproduce transient sodium activation midpoint, slope, and 10-90 percent rise together under the Ding, Wei, and Zhou SNr command protocol? |
| P2 | parameter | open | Which directly reported sodium transition rates reproduce the composite transient-current deactivation tail at minus 40 mV without breaking the accepted inactivation and biexponential recovery endpoints? |
| P3 | parameter | open | Which directly reported Kv3-like transition-rate equations reproduce complete-current deactivation tails at minus 60, minus 50, and minus 40 mV while preserving the accepted activation, inactivation, and rise endpoints? |
| W1 | wiring | open | Which coupled closed, open, fast-inactivated, slow-recovery, and blocked states are required by primary evidence for the SNr transient sodium current, and which transitions can be omitted without replacing biological channel dynamics with host logic? |
| W2 | wiring | open | Which Kv3.1 and Kv3.4 subunit or state composition is supported for SNr IDR-fast, and what coupled activation-inactivation topology is required to represent its measured current tails? |
